
## Initial acceptance checklist

- [ ] Registration entry page exists.
- [ ] Account-creation form matches API registration fields.
- [ ] Email-verification state has a dedicated UI.
- [ ] Pending, expired, invalid, and already-used states are distinct.
- [ ] Registration continuation/claim is ownership-bound.
- [ ] Plan-selection page uses server-owned plan data.
- [ ] Every visible plan has a matching backend plan or offer.
- [ ] Plan cards show only implemented capabilities.
- [ ] Pricing, currency, and billing period match the pricebook.
- [ ] Provider/API-key responsibility is stated consistently.
- [ ] Trial eligibility and duration match backend policy.
- [ ] Checkout actions map to real API routes.
- [ ] Subscription status maps to UI states.
- [ ] Entitlements and quotas are not shown as active before activation.
- [ ] Review-required and payment-pending states are fail-closed.
- [ ] Empty, loading, error, retry, and success states exist.
- [ ] Responsive and keyboard-accessible behavior is covered.
- [ ] UI contract tests cover pages, cards, labels, and actions.
- [ ] Full Python suite remains green.
